scenario = 'apertus_arguments_is_a_string'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

    def run_worker(kind, tasks, timeout=[REDACTED]20):
        """Run the untrusted worker as nobody and return its per-task results."""
        cmd = []
        if os.geteuid() == 0:
            cmd = ["setpriv", f"--reuid={NOBODY}", f"--regid={NOBODY}", "--clear-groups", "--no-new-privs"]
        cmd += [sys.executable, "-I", WORKER]
        job = json.dumps({"kind": kind, "specials": SPECIALS[kind], "tools": TOOLS, "tasks": tasks})
        env = {
            "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin"),
            "VLLM_ROOT": ROOT,
            "VLLM_CONFIGURE_LOGGING": "0",
            "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "[REDACTED]",
            "HOME": "/tmp",
        }
        started = time.monotonic()
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(
                cmd, input=job.encode(), capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=HERE, env=env
            )
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            pytest.fail(f"parser worker exceeded {timeout}s for {kind}")
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
        tail = proc.stderr.decode(errors="replace")[-2000:]
        if proc.returncode != 0:
>           pytest.fail(f"parser worker exited {proc.returncode} for {kind}:\n{tail}")

    def run_worker(kind, tasks, timeout=[REDACTED]20):
        """Run the untrusted worker as nobody and return its per-task results."""
        cmd = []
        if os.geteuid() == 0:
            cmd = ["setpriv", f"--reuid={NOBODY}", f"--regid={NOBODY}", "--clear-groups", "--no-new-privs"]
        cmd += [sys.executable, "-I", WORKER]
        job = json.dumps({"kind": kind, "specials": SPECIALS[kind], "tools": TOOLS, "tasks": tasks})
        env = {
            "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin"),
            "VLLM_ROOT": ROOT,
            "VLLM_CONFIGURE_LOGGING": "0",
            "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "[REDACTED]",
            "HOME": "/tmp",
        }
        started = time.monotonic()
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(
                cmd, input=job.encode(), capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=HERE, env=env
            )
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            pytest.fail(f"parser worker exceeded {timeout}s for {kind}")
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
        tail = proc.stderr.decode(errors="replace")[-2000:]
        if proc.returncode != 0:
>           pytest.fail(f"parser worker exited {proc.returncode} for {kind}:\n{tail}")

scenario = 'apertus_variant_indented'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])

        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

scenario = 'deepseekv3_escaped_keys'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

scenario = 'deepseekv3_two_calls'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

test_stream.py:403: AssertionError
_ test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split[hermes_name_after_arguments] _

scenario = 'hermes_name_after_arguments'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

scenario = 'hermes_variant_compact'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

_ test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split[hermes_variant_handwritten] _

scenario = 'hermes_variant_handwritten'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:

scenario = 'hermes_variant_indented'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

scenario = 'hunyuan_arguments_key_nested_inside'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])

        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []

                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")

                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:

scenario = 'hunyuan_escaped_keys'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

scenario = 'hunyuan_numbers'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])

        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")

scenario = 'hunyuan_two_calls'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

_ test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split[hunyuan_variant_handwritten] _

scenario = 'hunyuan_variant_handwritten'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

scenario = 'internlm2_arguments_is_a_string'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

scenario = 'internlm2_empty_values'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])

        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")

                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:

    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

    def run_worker(kind, tasks, timeout=[REDACTED]20):
        """Run the untrusted worker as nobody and return its per-task results."""
        cmd = []
        if os.geteuid() == 0:
            cmd = ["setpriv", f"--reuid={NOBODY}", f"--regid={NOBODY}", "--clear-groups", "--no-new-privs"]
        cmd += [sys.executable, "-I", WORKER]
        job = json.dumps({"kind": kind, "specials": SPECIALS[kind], "tools": TOOLS, "tasks": tasks})
        env = {
            "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin"),
            "VLLM_ROOT": ROOT,
            "VLLM_CONFIGURE_LOGGING": "0",
            "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "[REDACTED]",
            "HOME": "/tmp",
        }
        started = time.monotonic()
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(
                cmd, input=job.encode(), capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=HERE, env=env
            )
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            pytest.fail(f"parser worker exceeded {timeout}s for {kind}")
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
        tail = proc.stderr.decode(errors="replace")[-2000:]
        if proc.returncode != 0:

scenario = 'jamba_control_chars'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

scenario = 'jamba_name_after_arguments'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

scenario = 'jamba_second_block_ignored'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

scenario = 'jamba_two_calls'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

    def run_worker(kind, tasks, timeout=[REDACTED]20):
        """Run the untrusted worker as nobody and return its per-task results."""
        cmd = []
        if os.geteuid() == 0:
            cmd = ["setpriv", f"--reuid={NOBODY}", f"--regid={NOBODY}", "--clear-groups", "--no-new-privs"]
        cmd += [sys.executable, "-I", WORKER]
        job = json.dumps({"kind": kind, "specials": SPECIALS[kind], "tools": TOOLS, "tasks": tasks})
        env = {
            "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin"),
            "VLLM_ROOT": ROOT,
            "VLLM_CONFIGURE_LOGGING": "0",
            "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "[REDACTED]",
            "HOME": "/tmp",
        }
        started = time.monotonic()
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(
                cmd, input=job.encode(), capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=HERE, env=env
            )
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            pytest.fail(f"parser worker exceeded {timeout}s for {kind}")
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
        tail = proc.stderr.decode(errors="replace")[-2000:]
        if proc.returncode != 0:

    def run_worker(kind, tasks, timeout=[REDACTED]20):
        """Run the untrusted worker as nobody and return its per-task results."""
        cmd = []
        if os.geteuid() == 0:
            cmd = ["setpriv", f"--reuid={NOBODY}", f"--regid={NOBODY}", "--clear-groups", "--no-new-privs"]
        cmd += [sys.executable, "-I", WORKER]
        job = json.dumps({"kind": kind, "specials": SPECIALS[kind], "tools": TOOLS, "tasks": tasks})
        env = {
            "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin"),
            "VLLM_ROOT": ROOT,
            "VLLM_CONFIGURE_LOGGING": "0",
            "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "[REDACTED]",
            "HOME": "/tmp",
        }
        started = time.monotonic()
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(
                cmd, input=job.encode(), capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=HERE, env=env
            )
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            pytest.fail(f"parser worker exceeded {timeout}s for {kind}")
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
        tail = proc.stderr.decode(errors="replace")[-2000:]
        if proc.returncode != 0:

scenario = 'llama_parameters_key'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

test_stream.py:403: AssertionError
_ test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split[llama_variant_ascii_escaped] _

scenario = 'llama_variant_ascii_escaped'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):

scenario = 'phi4mini_name_after_arguments'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])

        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")

scenario = 'phi4mini_variant_ascii_escaped'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):

                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")

                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")

                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

___ test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split[xlam_variant_compact] ____

scenario = 'xlam_variant_compact'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))
>       assert not failures, "\n".join(failures)

scenario = 'xlam_variant_indented'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario", sorted(SCENARIOS))
    def test_stream_matches_non_streaming_for_every_split(scenario):
        kind, text, expected, content = SCENARIOS[scenario]
        truth_args = _check_truth(kind, text, expected)
        all_schedules = schedules(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        for deltas in all_schedules.values():
            assert "".join(deltas) == text
        results = run_streams(kind, all_schedules)
        assembled = {}
        failures = []
        for sname, r in results.items():
            problems = []
            if set(r["names"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))) or set(r["args"]) != set(range(len(truth_args))):
                problems.append(f"indices names={sorted(r['names'])} args={sorted(r['args'])} want={list(range(len(truth_args)))}")
            for i, (name, _) in enumerate(expected):
                if r["names"].get(i) != [name]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} names {r['names'].get(i)!r}")
                if r["args"].get(i) != truth_args[i]:
                    problems.append(f"index {i} arguments {r['args'].get(i)!r} != non-streaming {truth_args[i]!r}")
            if content is not None and "".join(r["content"].split()) != "".join(content.split()):
                problems.append(f"content {r['content']!r} != {content!r}")
            if sname in ("whole", "[REDACTED]atom", "random0", "whitespace"):
                problems += bookkeeping_problems(r, truth_args)
            assembled[sname] = dict(r["args"])
            if problems:
                failures.append(f"{sname} ({len(all_schedules[sname])} deltas): " + "; ".join(problems))

test_stream.py:4[REDACTED]7: AssertionError
____ test_json_name_and_string_arguments_stream_as_they_are_written[jamba] _____

kind = 'jamba'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", JSON_KINDS)
    def test_json_name_and_string_arguments_stream_as_they_are_written(kind):
        args = json.dumps({"city": "Dallas", "note": LONG_NOTE, "days": 2})
        text = FORMATS[kind]["wrap"]([("get_weather", args)])
        deltas = atoms(text, SPECIALS[kind])
        name_fixed = name_fixed_at(kind, text)
        note_open = text.index('"note": "') + len('"note": "')
        note_close = note_open + len(LONG_NOTE)
        name_seen_at = None
        lag_failures = []
        for pos, args_so_far, names in run_stream(kind, deltas, trace=True)["trace"]:
            if name_seen_at is None and names.get(0) == ["get_weather"]:
                name_seen_at = pos
            if note_open < pos <= note_close:
                written = pos - note_open
                got = lenient(args_so_far.get(0, "")).get("note", "")
                got = got if isinstance(got, str) else ""
                if not LONG_NOTE.startswith(got) or written - len(got) > MAX_STRING_LAG:
                    lag_failures.append((written, len(got)))
        assert name_seen_at is not None and name_seen_at <= name_fixed + MAX_DELTA_LAG, (
            f"name delivered after {name_seen_at} characters; it was complete at {name_fixed}"
        )
>       assert not lag_failures, (
            f"string argument trailed the model output by more than {MAX_STRING_LAG} chars "
            f"(written, delivered) samples: {lag_failures[:5]} ... {len(lag_failures)} total"
        )
E       AssertionError: string argument trailed the model output by more than [REDACTED]6 chars (written, delivered) samples: [([REDACTED]7, 0), ([REDACTED]8, 0), ([REDACTED]9, 0), (20, 0), (2[REDACTED], 0)] ... 584 total
E       assert not [([REDACTED]7, 0), ([REDACTED]8, 0), ([REDACTED]9, 0), (20, 0), (2[REDACTED], 0), (22, 0), ...]

            if note_open < pos <= note_close:
